  python -B scripts/check_frontmatter.py [--citations local_reports/frontmatter_inventory_2026-11-17/h4_citations.json]

Checks
  F1 format   : every tracked .md whose first line is '---' has a closed block, LF only, no BOM,
                keys id/kind/created/title present, id matches the ID Gate pattern, created is a real date.
  F2 unique   : each id is used by exactly one tracked file.
  F3 kind     : kind is one of the agreed vocabulary words.
  F4 body     : for files whose frontmatter was added later, the commit that added it changed nothing
                below the block (body bytes equal to the parent version).
  F5 exempt   : id_gate_baseline.json exempt_paths exist, lack an id, and every gated file without an id is exempt.
  F6 citations: (optional, local file) each shifted line citation points at the same text after the shift.

Exit code 0 = all checks passed, 1 = at least one finding. Findings are printed one per line.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime
import json
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
KINDS = {"draft", "review", "report", "note", "essay", "paper", "dialog", "dict", "guide", "exp", "spec", "index"}
REQUIRED = ("id", "kind", "created", "title")
# Reviewed 2026-11-20 with the owner. Kinds used before the rollout vocabulary existed (not rewritten).
LEGACY_KINDS = {
    "dictionary/NRA-IDE数式配置の5大鉄則（式の文法）.md": "dictionary-supplement",
    "note/03_実装・計算機アーキテクチャ/nra-ide-hybridcalc-quantum-architecture-next_ver/NRA_IDE_Quantum_Session_Records_EN.md": "record",
    "note/03_実装・計算機アーキテクチャ/nra-ide-hybridcalc-quantum-architecture-next_ver/NRA_IDE_Quantum_Session_Records_JP.md": "record",
    "note/03_実装・計算機アーキテクチャ/nra-ide-hybridcalc-quantum-architecture-next_ver/変遷記録.md": "record",
    "note/06_対話セッション記録/NRA-IDE_開いた動的構造式と泥臭い応用式の対照精査および正典v2.5整合報告_精査報告_2026-11-16.md": "review-report",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/AI_Studio.md": "poc-log",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/exploration_review_summary_20261010.md": "review-summary",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/gemini_possibility_exploration_assessment_20261010-1913.md": "exploration-record",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/仮説は、人工知能論や技術的特異点（シンギュラリティ）の議論において「知能爆発（Intelligence Explosion）」または「自己再帰的改善（Recursive Self-Improvement）」と呼ばれるモデル.md": "dialogue-log",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/可能性探索における記録・再検索・更新の方法20261010-1904.md": "method",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/理解と束縛の分離_着想20261012.md": "idea",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/相関票と多数決の限界_着想20261012.md": "idea",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/論点纏め_境界理解と検証構造_20261012.md": "summary",
    "note/07_知能爆発-自己再帰的改善/近接化の形と計算の切替_着想20261011.md": "idea",
    "note/08_セキュリティ/#_Microsoftが確認した事実と、まだ証明されていないこと（AIサイバー攻撃の実態）.md": "article",
    "nra-core/foundations/NRA-IDE_説明用拡張式体系.md": "explanatory-reference",
    "nra-core/foundations/開放系_境界接近比の増分分解_論文草案_精査報告_別観点第二精査（条件付き完了-1）_2026-11-16.md": "review-report",
    "obsidian-index/README.md": "doc",
}
# Reviewed 2026-11-20: commits that added frontmatter together with intended body edits
# (e0505e9 added six dictionary terms; the others are October edits made before the rollout).
ACCEPTED_BODY_CHANGES = {
    "dictionary/NRA-IDE_Dictionary_EN.md": "e0505e9",
    "dictionary/NRA-IDE_Dictionary_JP.md": "e0505e9",
    "note/06_対話セッション記録/AI社会の整備・修理能力と技能継承の断絶.md": "7b1cac1",
    "note/08_セキュリティ/#_Microsoftが確認した事実と、まだ証明されていないこと（AIサイバー攻撃の実態）.md": "1be1cc2",
    "note/AIリテラシー/HF事件/HF事件_今までのAIとは何が変わったのか_別視点_20260913-2114.md": "266f895",
    "note/AIリテラシー/HF事件/報酬ハッキング_1200体AI_HuggingFace事件_20260913-1830.md": "266f895",
}


def git(*args: str) -> bytes:
    return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, check=True).stdout


def split_block(raw: bytes):
    """Return (fields, body_bytes) or None when the file has no closed frontmatter block."""
    if not raw.startswith(b"---\n"):
        return None
    end = raw.find(b"\n---\n", 3)
    if end < 0:
        return None
    fields = {}
    for line in raw[4:end].decode("utf-8").split("\n"):
        if ":" in line and not line.startswith((" ", "-")):
            key, value = line.split(":", 1)
            fields[key.strip()] = value.split(" #")[0].strip().strip('"')
    body = raw[end + 5:]
    return fields, body[1:] if body.startswith(b"\n") else body


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--citations", type=Path)
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    policy = json.loads((ROOT / "id_gate_policy.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    baseline = json.loads((ROOT / "id_gate_baseline.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    id_pattern = re.compile(policy["id_pattern"])
    gated = tuple(policy["include_prefixes"])
    findings: list[str] = []
    notes: list[str] = []
    owners: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    with_fm = 0
    files = [p for p in git("ls-files", "-z", "*.md").decode("utf-8").split("\0") if p]

    for rel in files:
        raw = (ROOT / rel).read_bytes()
        if b"\r" in raw:
            # Judge the committed state; a CRLF working copy over an LF index is only a local note.
            indexed = git("show", f":{rel}")
            if b"\r" not in indexed:
                notes.append(f"W1 WORKTREE_CRLF {rel}")
                raw = indexed
        if raw.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf---") or (raw.startswith(b"---") and b"\r" in raw[:400]):
            findings.append(f"F1 BOM_OR_CRLF {rel}")
            continue
        block = split_block(raw)
        if raw.startswith(b"---\n") and block is None:
            findings.append(f"F1 UNCLOSED {rel}")
            continue
        if block is None:
            continue
        fields, _ = block
        if not fields.get("id"):
            continue
        with_fm += 1
        for key in REQUIRED:
            if not fields.get(key):
                findings.append(f"F1 MISSING_{key.upper()} {rel}")
        if not id_pattern.fullmatch(fields["id"]):
            findings.append(f"F1 BAD_ID {rel} {fields['id']}")
        try:
            datetime.date.fromisoformat(fields.get("created", ""))
        except ValueError:
            findings.append(f"F1 BAD_CREATED {rel} {fields.get('created')}")
        if fields.get("kind") not in KINDS and LEGACY_KINDS.get(rel) != fields.get("kind"):
            findings.append(f"F3 KIND {rel} {fields.get('kind')}")
        owners.setdefault(fields["id"], []).append(rel)

    for ident, paths in owners.items():
        if len(paths) > 1:
            findings.append(f"F2 DUPLICATE {ident} " + " ".join(paths))

    # F4: find the commit that first gave the file a frontmatter block and compare bodies with its parent.
    checked_f4 = 0
    for ident, paths in owners.items():
        rel = paths[0]
        log = git("log", "--format=%H", "--follow", "--", rel).decode().split()
        added = None
        for commit in reversed(log):  # oldest first
            try:
                blob = git("show", f"{commit}:{rel}")
            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
                continue
            if split_block(blob) and split_block(blob)[0].get("id"):
                added = (commit, blob)
                break
        if not added or added[0] == log[-1]:
            continue  # created with frontmatter from the start
        try:
            parent = git("show", f"{added[0]}^:{rel}")
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
            continue  # renamed in the same commit; skip
        checked_f4 += 1
        if split_block(added[1])[1] != parent and not added[0].startswith(ACCEPTED_BODY_CHANGES.get(rel, "-")):
            findings.append(f"F4 BODY_CHANGED {rel} at {added[0][:7]}")

    exempt = set(baseline["exempt_paths"])
    tracked = set(files)
    for rel in sorted(exempt):
        if rel not in tracked:
            findings.append(f"F5 EXEMPT_MISSING {rel}")
        elif rel.endswith(".md") and any(rel in p for p in owners.values()):
            findings.append(f"F5 EXEMPT_HAS_ID {rel}")
    has_id = {p for paths in owners.values() for p in paths}
    for rel in files:
        if rel.startswith(gated) and rel not in has_id and rel not in exempt:
            findings.append(f"F5 GATED_WITHOUT_ID {rel}")

    checked_f6 = 0
    if args.citations:
        rows = json.loads(args.citations.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for row in rows:
            target = row["target"]
            now = (ROOT / target).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
            log = git("log", "--format=%H", "--", target).decode().split()
            before = None
            for commit in log:  # newest first; first version without frontmatter
                blob = git("show", f"{commit}:{target}")
                if not blob.startswith(b"---\n"):
                    before = blob.decode("utf-8").splitlines()
                    break
            if before is None:
                findings.append(f"F6 NO_PRE_VERSION {target}")
                continue
            for old, new in zip(range(row["old"][0], row["old"][1] + 1), range(row["new"][0], row["new"][1] + 1)):
                checked_f6 += 1
                if old > len(before) or new > len(now) or before[old - 1] != now[new - 1]:
                    findings.append(f"F6 SHIFT_MISMATCH {target} {old}->{new} (cited by {row['source']}:{row['source_line']})")

    for f in findings + notes:
        print(f)
    print(f"SUMMARY files={len(files)} with_id={with_fm} ids={len(owners)} body_checked={checked_f4} "
          f"citation_lines={checked_f6} findings={len(findings)} notes={len(notes)}")
    return 1 if findings else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
scripts/check_links.pyGitHub 原文
"""Check internal links in root README.md and README_JP.md."""
import re
import os
import sys

TARGET_FILES = ["README.md", "README_JP.md"]
LINK_RE = re.compile(r'\[.*?\]\(([^)]+)\)')

errors = []

for readme in TARGET_FILES:
    content = open(readme, encoding="utf-8").read()
    for raw in LINK_RE.findall(content):
        path = raw.split("#")[0].strip()
        if not path or path.startswith("http") or path.startswith("mailto"):
            continue
        path = path.lstrip("./")
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            errors.append(f"{readme}: broken → {path}")

if errors:
    for e in errors:
        print(f"FAIL {e}")
    sys.exit(1)

print(f"OK  internal links ({', '.join(TARGET_FILES)})")
scripts/check_path_case.pyGitHub 原文
"""Check that every Git-tracked path matches the on-disk letter case exactly.

Windows and default macOS filesystems are case-insensitive, so a path with the
wrong case still opens successfully there. Linux CI runners are case-sensitive,
so a mismatch between the case Git recorded and the actual case on disk will
break `open()`, imports, and links only after checkout on Linux. This check
catches that class of bug locally, before it reaches CI.
"""
import subprocess
import sys
import os


def tracked_paths() -> list[str]:
    output = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "ls-files"],
        capture_output=True,
        check=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
    ).stdout
    return [line for line in output.splitlines() if line]


def actual_case(path: str) -> str | None:
    """Return the on-disk path with real casing, or None if it cannot be resolved."""
    parts = path.split("/")
    current = "."
    resolved = []
    for part in parts:
        if not os.path.isdir(current) and current != ".":
            return None
        try:
            entries = os.listdir(current)
        except OSError:
            return None
        match = next((entry for entry in entries if entry.lower() == part.lower()), None)
        if match is None:
            return None
        resolved.append(match)
        current = os.path.join(current, match)
    return "/".join(resolved)


def main() -> int:
    errors = []
    for path in tracked_paths():
        real = actual_case(path)
        if real is None:
            # Deleted-but-still-tracked or submodule-like path; not this check's concern.
            continue
        if real != path:
            errors.append(f"{path}  (on disk: {real})")

    if errors:
        print("FAIL path case mismatch between Git and the filesystem:")
        for e in errors:
            print(f"  {e}")
        print(
            "\nThese paths will fail to open on case-sensitive filesystems "
            "(Linux CI, most macOS setups) even though they work on Windows."
        )
        return 1

    print(f"OK  path case matches for {len(tracked_paths())} tracked files")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
scripts/fileop_gate.pyGitHub 原文
"""Require a file-operation report when a pushed range deletes or renames files.

AGENTS.md §8「ファイル操作の報告」と skills/file-operation-report/SKILL.md の機械的ゲート。

強制できるのは削除（D）と改名（R）だけである。コピーは追加（A）、上書きは変更（M）として
現れるため通常の編集と区別できず、未追跡ファイルと作業ルート外の操作はGitから見えない。
この範囲の限界は規則の文面で補う。

報告書はパスを`AI-IDE-NRA`配下の形（例`NRA-IDE/theory/AXIOMS.md`）で書くため、
Gitが返すリポジトリ相対パス（例`theory/AXIOMS.md`）の部分一致で照合する。

frontmatterの解析規則は`id_gate.py`と同じだが、`python -I`（isolated modeでは
スクリプトのディレクトリがsys.pathに入らない）でも動くよう、ここに複製する。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import pathlib
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Mapping, Sequence


EXIT_PASS = 0
EXIT_FINDINGS = 1
EXIT_GATE_ERROR = 2

REPORT_PREFIX = "audit/file_operations/"
REPORT_EXTENSION = ".md"
REQUIRED_KEYS = ("id", "kind", "created", "title")
REPORT_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"REPORT-FILEOP-\d{8}-\d{4}")


class GateError(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Finding:
    rule: str
    path: str
    detail: str


def run_git(repo: pathlib.Path, *arguments: str) -> bytes:
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "-C", str(repo), *arguments],
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
        check=False,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        message = result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
        raise GateError(f"git {' '.join(arguments[:2])} failed: {message}")
    return result.stdout


def parse_frontmatter(text: str) -> dict[str, str] | None:
    """Return the top-level `key: value` pairs of a leading `---` block, or None."""
    lines = text.lstrip(chr(0xFEFF)).splitlines()
    if not lines or lines[0].strip() != "---":
        return None
    values: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in lines[1:]:
        if line.strip() == "---":
            return values
        match = re.match(r"^([A-Za-z_][\w-]*):\s*(.*?)\s*$", line)
        if match:
            value = re.sub(r"\s+#.*$", "", match.group(2)).strip()
            values[match.group(1)] = value.strip("\"'")
    return None


def is_report(path: str) -> bool:
    return path.startswith(REPORT_PREFIX) and path.endswith(REPORT_EXTENSION)


def parse_name_status(raw: bytes) -> tuple[list[str], list[tuple[str, str]]]:
    """Split `--name-status -z` output into deleted paths and (old, new) rename pairs.

    A status field is followed by one path, except R/C which carry two.
    """
    tokens = [item.decode("utf-8") for item in raw.split(b"\0") if item]
    deleted: list[str] = []
    renamed: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    index = 0
    while index < len(tokens):
        status = tokens[index]
        if status[:1] in ("R", "C"):
            if index + 2 >= len(tokens):
                raise GateError("malformed name-status output")
            renamed.append((tokens[index + 1], tokens[index + 2]))
            index += 3
            continue
        if index + 1 >= len(tokens):
            raise GateError("malformed name-status output")
        if status[:1] == "D":
            deleted.append(tokens[index + 1])
        index += 2
    return deleted, renamed


def mentions(text: str, path: str) -> bool:
    """A report may write the path with either separator."""
    return path in text or path.replace("/", "\\") in text


def read_reports(repo: pathlib.Path, head: str, paths: Sequence[str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Read report blobs by object id; `git show rev:path` fails on long Windows paths."""
    wanted = set(paths)
    oids: dict[str, str] = {}
    for entry in run_git(repo, "ls-tree", "-r", "-z", head).split(b"\0"):
        if not entry:
            continue
        meta, _, raw_path = entry.partition(b"\t")
        path = raw_path.decode("utf-8")
        if path in wanted:
            oids[path] = meta.split()[2].decode("ascii")
    contents: dict[str, str] = {}
    for path, oid in oids.items():
        blob = run_git(repo, "cat-file", "blob", oid)
        try:
            contents[path] = blob.decode("utf-8")
        except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
            raise GateError(f"not valid UTF-8: {path}") from error
    return contents


def check_reports(reports: Mapping[str, str]) -> list[Finding]:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    for path in sorted(reports):
        frontmatter = parse_frontmatter(reports[path])
        if frontmatter is None:
            findings.append(Finding("NO_FRONTMATTER", path, "frontmatter (--- block at line 1) is missing"))
            continue
        for key in REQUIRED_KEYS:
            if not frontmatter.get(key):
                findings.append(Finding("MISSING_KEY", path, f"required key is missing or empty: {key}"))
        identifier = frontmatter.get("id", "")
        if identifier and not REPORT_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(identifier):
            findings.append(Finding("BAD_REPORT_ID", path,
                                    f"id does not match {REPORT_ID_PATTERN.pattern}: {identifier}"))
    return findings


def evaluate_range(repo: pathlib.Path, base: str, head: str):
    """Return the affected paths, the reports added in the range, and the findings."""
    deleted, renamed = parse_name_status(
        run_git(repo, "diff", "--name-status", "-z", "-M", "--diff-filter=DR", base, head)
    )
    # 50 requires both sides of a move; a rename with detection off arrives as D plus A.
    # 過去の報告書の削除・改名も対象に含める。除外すると記録なしで記録を消せる。
    affected = sorted({*deleted, *(old for old, _ in renamed), *(new for _, new in renamed)})

    added = [
        item.decode("utf-8")
        for item in run_git(repo, "diff", "--name-only", "-z", "-M", "--diff-filter=AMR", base, head).split(b"\0")
        if item
    ]
    report_paths = sorted(path for path in added if is_report(path))

    findings: list[Finding] = []
    if not affected:
        return affected, {}, findings

    reports = read_reports(repo, head, report_paths)
    if not reports:
        findings.append(Finding(
            "MISSING_REPORT", REPORT_PREFIX,
            f"{len(affected)} path(s) deleted or renamed, but no report was added under {REPORT_PREFIX}",
        ))
        return affected, reports, findings

    findings.extend(check_reports(reports))
    for path in affected:
        if not any(mentions(text, path) for text in reports.values()):
            findings.append(Finding("PATH_NOT_REPORTED", path,
                                    "deleted or renamed path is not named in any added report"))
    return affected, reports, findings


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Check that pushed deletions and renames carry a file-operation report."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--repo", type=pathlib.Path, default=pathlib.Path.cwd())
    parser.add_argument("--base")
    parser.add_argument("--head", default="HEAD")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
    repo = arguments.repo.resolve()

    try:
        if not arguments.base:
            raise GateError("--base is required")
        affected, reports, findings = evaluate_range(repo, arguments.base, arguments.head)
    except GateError as error:
        print(f"FILEOP_GATE: error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return EXIT_GATE_ERROR

    if arguments.json:
        print(json.dumps({"schema_version": 1, "status": "FAIL" if findings else "PASS",
                          "scanned_files": len(affected), "report_files": len(reports),
                          "base": arguments.base, "head": arguments.head,
                          "findings": [asdict(f) for f in findings]}, ensure_ascii=True))
        return EXIT_FINDINGS if findings else EXIT_PASS
    print(f"FILEOP_GATE: checked {len(affected)} deleted/renamed path(s) against {len(reports)} report(s)")
    for finding in findings:
        print(json.dumps(asdict(finding)))
    if findings:
        print(f"FILEOP_GATE: FAIL ({len(findings)} finding(s))", file=sys.stderr)
        return EXIT_FINDINGS
    print("FILEOP_GATE: PASS")
    return EXIT_PASS


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
scripts/id_gate.pyGitHub 原文
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import pathlib
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from typing import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence


EXIT_PASS = 0
EXIT_FINDINGS = 1
EXIT_GATE_ERROR = 2

MISSING_RULES = {"NO_FRONTMATTER", "MISSING_KEY", "EMPTY_ID"}


class GateError(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Policy:
    include_prefixes: tuple[str, ...]
    extensions: tuple[str, ...]
    required_keys: tuple[str, ...]
    id_pattern: re.Pattern[str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Baseline:
    exempt_paths: frozenset[str]
    known_unresolved_related: frozenset[str]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Finding:
    rule: str
    path: str
    detail: str


def load_policy(path: pathlib.Path) -> Policy:
    try:
        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if raw["schema_version"] != 1:
            raise GateError("unsupported policy schema_version")
        return Policy(
            include_prefixes=tuple(raw["include_prefixes"]),
            extensions=tuple(raw["extensions"]),
            required_keys=tuple(raw["required_keys"]),
            id_pattern=re.compile(raw["id_pattern"]),
        )
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, re.error) as error:
        raise GateError(f"policy load failed: {error}") from error


def load_baseline(path: pathlib.Path | None) -> Baseline:
    if path is None:
        return Baseline(frozenset(), frozenset())
    try:
        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return Baseline(
            exempt_paths=frozenset(raw.get("exempt_paths", [])),
            known_unresolved_related=frozenset(raw.get("known_unresolved_related", [])),
        )
    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError) as error:
        raise GateError(f"baseline load failed: {error}") from error


def is_target(path: str, policy: Policy) -> bool:
    return path.startswith(policy.include_prefixes) and path.endswith(policy.extensions)


def parse_frontmatter(text: str) -> dict[str, str] | None:
    """Return the top-level `key: value` pairs of a leading `---` block, or None."""
    lines = text.lstrip(chr(0xFEFF)).splitlines()
    if not lines or lines[0].strip() != "---":
        return None
    values: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in lines[1:]:
        if line.strip() == "---":
            return values
        match = re.match(r"^([A-Za-z_][\w-]*):\s*(.*?)\s*$", line)
        if match:
            value = re.sub(r"\s+#.*$", "", match.group(2)).strip()
            values[match.group(1)] = value.strip("\"'")
    return None


def parse_list(value: str) -> list[str]:
    inner = value.strip()
    if inner.startswith("[") and inner.endswith("]"):
        inner = inner[1:-1]
    return [item.strip().strip("\"'") for item in inner.split(",") if item.strip()]


def check_file(
    path: str,
    text: str,
    policy: Policy,
    baseline: Baseline,
) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, str] | None]:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    frontmatter = parse_frontmatter(text)
    exempt = path in baseline.exempt_paths
    if frontmatter is None:
        if not exempt:
            findings.append(Finding("NO_FRONTMATTER", path, "frontmatter (--- block at line 1) is missing"))
        return findings, None
    for key in policy.required_keys:
        if not frontmatter.get(key):
            rule = "EMPTY_ID" if key == "id" else "MISSING_KEY"
            if not exempt:
                findings.append(Finding(rule, path, f"required key is missing or empty: {key}"))
    identifier = frontmatter.get("id", "")
    if identifier and not policy.id_pattern.fullmatch(identifier):
        findings.append(Finding("BAD_ID_FORMAT", path, f"id does not match {policy.id_pattern.pattern}: {identifier}"))
    return findings, frontmatter


def evaluate(
    changed: Iterable[str],
    contents: Mapping[str, str],
    policy: Policy,
    baseline: Baseline,
) -> list[Finding]:
    """`contents` holds every target file in the head tree; `changed` is the pushed subset."""
    parsed: dict[str, dict[str, str] | None] = {}
    owners: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    for path, text in contents.items():
        frontmatter = parse_frontmatter(text)
        parsed[path] = frontmatter
        if frontmatter and frontmatter.get("id"):
            owners.setdefault(frontmatter["id"], []).append(path)

    findings: list[Finding] = []
    for path in sorted(set(changed)):
        if path not in contents:
            continue
        file_findings, frontmatter = check_file(path, contents[path], policy, baseline)
        findings.extend(file_findings)
        if not frontmatter:
            continue
        identifier = frontmatter.get("id", "")
        if identifier and len(owners.get(identifier, [])) > 1:
            others = [item for item in owners[identifier] if item != path]
            findings.append(Finding("DUPLICATE_ID", path, f"id {identifier} is also used by: {', '.join(others)}"))
        for reference in parse_list(frontmatter.get("related", "")):
            if reference not in owners and reference not in baseline.known_unresolved_related:
                findings.append(Finding("UNRESOLVED_RELATED", path, f"related id is not defined anywhere: {reference}"))
    return findings


def run_git(repo: pathlib.Path, *arguments: str, stdin: bytes | None = None) -> bytes:
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "-C", str(repo), *arguments],
        input=stdin,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
        check=False,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        message = result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip()
        raise GateError(f"git {' '.join(arguments[:2])} failed: {message}")
    return result.stdout


def split_paths(raw: bytes) -> list[str]:
    return [item.decode("utf-8") for item in raw.split(b"\0") if item]


def read_blobs(repo: pathlib.Path, oids: Mapping[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Read blobs by object id; `git show rev:path` fails on very long Windows paths."""
    contents: dict[str, str] = {}
    for path, oid in oids.items():
        blob = run_git(repo, "cat-file", "blob", oid)
        try:
            contents[path] = blob.decode("utf-8")
        except UnicodeDecodeError as error:
            raise GateError(f"not valid UTF-8: {path}") from error
    return contents


def collect(repo: pathlib.Path, base: str, head: str, policy: Policy) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, str]]:
    changed = [
        item
        for item in split_paths(run_git(repo, "diff", "--name-only", "-z", "--diff-filter=AMR", base, head))
        if is_target(item, policy)
    ]
    oids: dict[str, str] = {}
    for entry in run_git(repo, "ls-tree", "-r", "-z", head).split(b"\0"):
        if not entry:
            continue
        meta, _, raw_path = entry.partition(b"\t")
        path = raw_path.decode("utf-8")
        if is_target(path, policy):
            oids[path] = meta.split()[2].decode("ascii")
    return changed, read_blobs(repo, oids)


def evaluate_range(repo: pathlib.Path, base: str, head: str, policy: Policy, baseline: Baseline):
    changed, contents = collect(repo, base, head, policy)
    findings = evaluate(changed, contents, policy, baseline)
    _, old_contents = collect(repo, base, base, policy)
    def identifiers(values):
        return {fm["id"] for text in values.values() if (fm := parse_frontmatter(text)) and fm.get("id")}
    lost = identifiers(old_contents) - identifiers(contents)
    # An ID deletion/rename can break unchanged callers; do not enforce new
    # frontmatter rules on those callers while checking the affected references.
    for path, text in contents.items():
        if path in changed:
            continue
        fm = parse_frontmatter(text)
        for ref in parse_list(fm.get("related", "")) if fm else []:
            if ref in lost and ref not in baseline.known_unresolved_related:
                findings.append(Finding("UNRESOLVED_RELATED", path, f"related id was removed: {ref}"))
    return changed, contents, findings


def write_baseline(repo: pathlib.Path, policy: Policy, destination: pathlib.Path) -> int:
    """Record tracked working-tree files that currently lack required frontmatter."""
    tracked = [item for item in split_paths(run_git(repo, "ls-files", "-z")) if is_target(item, policy)]
    contents: dict[str, str] = {}
    for path in tracked:
        contents[path] = (repo / path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    empty = Baseline(frozenset(), frozenset())
    exempt = sorted(
        path
        for path, text in contents.items()
        if any(item.rule in MISSING_RULES for item in check_file(path, text, policy, empty)[0])
    )
    ids = {
        frontmatter["id"]
        for text in contents.values()
        if (frontmatter := parse_frontmatter(text)) and frontmatter.get("id")
    }
    unresolved = sorted(
        {
            reference
            for text in contents.values()
            if (frontmatter := parse_frontmatter(text))
            for reference in parse_list(frontmatter.get("related", ""))
            if reference not in ids
        }
    )
    payload = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "note": "exempt_paths may lack frontmatter until edited with the owner's permission; remove a path once it has an id.",
        "exempt_paths": exempt,
        "known_unresolved_related": unresolved,
    }
    destination.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    return len(exempt)


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Check that pushed Markdown records carry a unique frontmatter id.")
    parser.add_argument("--repo", type=pathlib.Path, default=pathlib.Path.cwd())
    parser.add_argument("--policy", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("--baseline", type=pathlib.Path)
    parser.add_argument("--base")
    parser.add_argument("--head", default="HEAD")
    parser.add_argument("--write-baseline", type=pathlib.Path, metavar="PATH")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
    arguments = parser.parse_args(argv)
    repo = arguments.repo.resolve()
    policy_path = arguments.policy or repo / "id_gate_policy.json"

    try:
        policy = load_policy(policy_path)
        if arguments.write_baseline:
            count = write_baseline(repo, policy, arguments.write_baseline)
            print(f"ID_GATE: baseline written, {count} path(s) exempt")
            return EXIT_PASS
        if not arguments.base:
            raise GateError("--base is required")
        baseline = load_baseline(arguments.baseline)
        changed, contents, findings = evaluate_range(repo, arguments.base, arguments.head, policy, baseline)
    except GateError as error:
        print(f"ID_GATE: error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return EXIT_GATE_ERROR

    if arguments.json:
        print(json.dumps({"schema_version": 1, "status": "FAIL" if findings else "PASS",
                          "scanned_files": len(contents), "changed_files": len(changed),
                          "base": arguments.base, "head": arguments.head,
                          "findings": [asdict(f) for f in findings]}, ensure_ascii=True))
        return EXIT_FINDINGS if findings else EXIT_PASS
    print(f"ID_GATE: checked {len(changed)} changed file(s) of {len(contents)} target file(s)")
    for finding in findings:
        print(json.dumps(asdict(finding)))
    if findings:
        print(f"ID_GATE: FAIL ({len(findings)} finding(s))", file=sys.stderr)
        return EXIT_FINDINGS
    print("ID_GATE: PASS")
    return EXIT_PASS


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
scripts/malware_gate.pyGitHub 原文
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import pathlib
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Sequence


EXIT_PASS = 0
EXIT_GATE_ERROR = 2
LFS_POINTER_PREFIX = b"version https://git-lfs.github.com/spec/v1\n"


class GateError(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass
class BlobRecord:
    oid: str
    size: int
    suffix: str
    paths: set[str]


def ascii_escape(value: str) -> str:
    return value.encode("unicode_escape", errors="backslashreplace").decode("ascii")


def run_git(repo: pathlib.Path, arguments: Sequence[str], input_bytes: bytes | None = None) -> bytes:
    try:
        return subprocess.check_output(
            ["git", *arguments],
            cwd=repo,
            input=input_bytes,
            stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
        )
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
        detail = exc.stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="backslashreplace").strip()
        raise GateError(
            f"git {' '.join(arguments)} failed with {exc.returncode}: {ascii_escape(detail)}"
        ) from exc


def resolve_commit(repo: pathlib.Path, revision: str) -> str:
    value = run_git(repo, ["rev-parse", "--verify", f"{revision}^{{commit}}"]).strip()
    try:
        oid = value.decode("ascii")
    except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:
        raise GateError("git returned a non-ASCII object identifier") from exc
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-fA-F]{40,64}", oid):
        raise GateError(f"invalid commit object identifier: {ascii_escape(oid)}")
    return oid.lower()


def outgoing_commits(repo: pathlib.Path, base_oid: str, head_oid: str) -> list[str]:
    raw = run_git(repo, ["rev-list", "--reverse", f"{base_oid}..{head_oid}"])
    commits = [line.decode("ascii").lower() for line in raw.splitlines() if line]
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40,64}", commit) for commit in commits):
        raise GateError("git rev-list returned an invalid object identifier")
    return commits


def newly_reachable_blobs(repo: pathlib.Path, base_oid: str, head_oid: str) -> dict[str, int]:
    raw_objects = run_git(
        repo,
        ["rev-list", "--objects", "--no-object-names", f"{base_oid}..{head_oid}"],
    )
    object_ids = [line.decode("ascii").lower() for line in raw_objects.splitlines() if line]
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40,64}", oid) for oid in object_ids):
        raise GateError("git rev-list returned an invalid reachable object identifier")
    if not object_ids:
        return {}
    batch_input = ("\n".join(object_ids) + "\n").encode("ascii")
    raw_metadata = run_git(
        repo,
        ["cat-file", "--batch-check=%(objectname) %(objecttype) %(objectsize)"],
        input_bytes=batch_input,
    )
    blobs: dict[str, int] = {}
    for line in raw_metadata.splitlines():
        parts = line.split(b" ")
        if len(parts) != 3:
            raise GateError("git cat-file returned incomplete object metadata")
        oid_bytes, object_type, size_bytes = parts
        try:
            oid = oid_bytes.decode("ascii").lower()
            size = int(size_bytes.decode("ascii"))
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise GateError("git cat-file returned invalid object metadata") from exc
        if object_type == b"blob":
            blobs[oid] = size
    return blobs


def safe_suffix(path: str) -> str:
    suffix = pathlib.PurePosixPath(path).suffix.lower()
    return suffix if re.fullmatch(r"\.[a-z0-9]{1,12}", suffix) else ".blob"


def tree_blobs(repo: pathlib.Path, commit_oid: str) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, str]], int]:
    raw = run_git(repo, ["ls-tree", "-r", "-z", commit_oid])
    blobs: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    gitlinks = 0
    for record in raw.split(b"\0"):
        if not record:
            continue
        try:
            header, path_bytes = record.split(b"\t", 1)
            mode, object_type, oid_bytes = header.split(b" ", 2)
            path = path_bytes.decode("utf-8")
            oid = oid_bytes.decode("ascii").lower()
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise GateError("git ls-tree returned an undecodable record") from exc
        if object_type == b"commit":
            gitlinks += 1
            continue
        if object_type != b"blob":
            raise GateError(
                f"unexpected tree object type {ascii_escape(object_type.decode('ascii', 'replace'))}"
            )
        if mode not in {b"100644", b"100755", b"120000"}:
            raise GateError(f"unexpected blob mode {ascii_escape(mode.decode('ascii', 'replace'))}")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{40,64}", oid):
            raise GateError("git ls-tree returned an invalid blob identifier")
        blobs.append((oid, path))
    return blobs, gitlinks


def tree_entries(repo: pathlib.Path, commit: str) -> dict[str, tuple[str, str]]:
    """Keep modes and original paths, including gitlinks; never follow links."""
    entries = {}
    for item in run_git(repo, ["ls-tree", "-r", "-z", commit]).split(b"\0"):
        if not item:
            continue
        meta, name = item.split(b"\t", 1)
        mode, kind, oid = meta.decode("ascii").split()
        path = name.decode("utf-8")
        if mode not in {"100644", "100755", "120000", "160000"}:
            raise GateError("unsupported Git file mode")
        if kind != ("commit" if mode == "160000" else "blob"):
            raise GateError("unexpected Git tree object type")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})", oid):
            raise GateError("invalid Git tree object id")
        entries[path] = (mode, oid)
    return entries


def materialize_outgoing_blobs(
    repo: pathlib.Path,
    base: str,
    head: str,
    output: pathlib.Path,
    maximum_total_bytes: int = 2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Materialize each unique blob newly reachable from outgoing commits.

    ``repo`` supplies the Git object database, ``commits`` the exact outgoing
    commit set, and ``output`` the destination directory. The cumulative blob
    size must not exceed ``maximum_total_bytes``. Existing output entries,
    malformed Git data, path collisions, or limit violations raise
    ``GateError``; the returned manifest describes only successfully written
    blobs.
    """
    repo = repo.resolve()
    output = output.resolve()
    if repo not in output.parents:
        raise GateError("extraction must remain inside the repository")
    if not (repo / ".git").exists():
        raise GateError(f"not a Git working tree: {ascii_escape(str(repo))}")
    if type(maximum_total_bytes) is not int or maximum_total_bytes <= 0:
        raise GateError("maximum_total_bytes must be positive")
    base_oid = resolve_commit(repo, base)
    head_oid = resolve_commit(repo, head)
    commits = outgoing_commits(repo, base_oid, head_oid)
    new_blobs = newly_reachable_blobs(repo, base_oid, head_oid)
    records: dict[str, BlobRecord] = {
        oid: BlobRecord(oid=oid, size=size, suffix=".blob", paths=set())
        for oid, size in new_blobs.items()
    }
    gitlinks = 0
    occurrences = []
    cache: dict[str, dict[str, tuple[str, str]]] = {}
    def tree(commit):
        if commit not in cache:
            cache[commit] = tree_entries(repo, commit)
        return cache[commit]
    for commit_oid in commits:
        parents = run_git(repo, ["rev-list", "--parents", "-n", "1", commit_oid]).decode("ascii").split()[1:]
        parent_trees = [tree(parent) for parent in parents]
        for path, (mode, oid) in tree(commit_oid).items():
            changed = not parents or any(t.get(path) != (mode, oid) for t in parent_trees)
            if mode == "160000":
                gitlinks += 1
                if changed:
                    raise GateError("changed gitlink requires separate submodule scanning")
                continue
            if oid not in new_blobs and not changed:
                continue
            if oid not in records:
                size = int(run_git(repo, ["cat-file", "-s", oid]).strip())
                records[oid] = BlobRecord(oid, size, safe_suffix(path), set())
            record = records[oid]
            if not record.paths:
                record.suffix = safe_suffix(path)
            record.paths.add(path)
            occurrences.append({"commit": commit_oid, "path": path, "mode": mode, "oid": oid})

    # Reject known over-limit input before loading even the first blob into RAM.
    if any(record.size < 0 for record in records.values()) or sum(r.size for r in records.values()) > maximum_total_bytes:
        raise GateError(f"outgoing blob bytes exceed limit {maximum_total_bytes}")
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.mkdir(parents=False, exist_ok=False)
    av_output = output / "av"
    av_output.mkdir()
    hashes = {}

    total_bytes = 0
    for oid in sorted(records):
        raw = run_git(repo, ["cat-file", "blob", oid])
        if raw.startswith(LFS_POINTER_PREFIX):
            raise GateError(
                f"Git LFS pointer {oid} requires separate LFS object scanning; refusing to continue"
            )
        total_bytes += len(raw)
        if total_bytes > maximum_total_bytes:
            raise GateError(
                f"outgoing blob bytes exceed limit {maximum_total_bytes}; refusing to skip content"
            )
        verified_oid = run_git(repo, ["hash-object", "--stdin"], input_bytes=raw).strip().decode("ascii")
        if verified_oid.lower() != oid:
            raise GateError(f"materialized blob hash mismatch for {oid}")
        record = records[oid]
        if record.size != len(raw):
            raise GateError(f"materialized blob size mismatch for {oid}")
        destination = output / f"{oid}{record.suffix}"
        with destination.open("xb") as handle:
            handle.write(raw)
        if oid in new_blobs:
            # Task-owned hardlinks avoid a second full copy of outgoing bytes.
            # Failure is an unavailable scan, never a reason to scan the worktree.
            os.link(destination, av_output / destination.name)
        hashes[oid] = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()

    digest_input = "".join(f"{oid} {records[oid].size}\n" for oid in sorted(records))
    blob_set_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(digest_input.encode("ascii")).hexdigest()
    manifest: dict[str, object] = {
        "schema_version": 2,
        "base_commit": base_oid,
        "head_commit": head_oid,
        "outgoing_commits": commits,
        "outgoing_commit_count": len(commits),
        "unique_blob_count": len(records),
        "total_blob_bytes": total_bytes,
        "blob_set_sha256": blob_set_sha256,
        "gitlink_occurrences": gitlinks,
        "av_blob_count": len(new_blobs),
        "av_directory": "av",
        "occurrences": occurrences,
        "unicode_input_count": len({(o["path"], o["oid"], o["mode"]) for o in occurrences}),
        "blobs": [
            {
                "oid": oid,
                "size": records[oid].size,
                "materialized_name": f"{oid}{records[oid].suffix}",
                "paths": sorted(records[oid].paths),
                "sha256": hashes[oid],
                "newly_reachable": oid in new_blobs,
            }
            for oid in sorted(records)
        ],
    }
    (output / "manifest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n",
        encoding="ascii",
        newline="\n",
    )
    return manifest


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Materialize every unique Git blob reachable from each outgoing commit"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--repo", type=pathlib.Path, required=True, help="Git repository to inspect"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--base", required=True, help="base revision excluded from the outgoing range"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--head", default="HEAD", help="range tip to inspect (default: HEAD)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output",
        type=pathlib.Path,
        required=True,
        help="directory where unique outgoing blobs and the manifest are materialized",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--maximum-total-bytes",
        type=int,
        default=2 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024,
        help="maximum total materialized blob bytes (default: 2147483648)",
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        manifest = materialize_outgoing_blobs(
            args.repo,
            args.base,
            args.head,
            args.output,
            args.maximum_total_bytes,
        )
    except (GateError, OSError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        print(f"malware-gate preparation failed: {ascii_escape(str(exc))}", file=sys.stderr)
        return EXIT_GATE_ERROR
    summary = {
        key: manifest[key]
        for key in (
            "schema_version",
            "base_commit",
            "head_commit",
            "outgoing_commit_count",
            "unique_blob_count",
            "total_blob_bytes",
            "blob_set_sha256",
            "gitlink_occurrences",
        )
    }
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2, sort_keys=True))
    return EXIT_PASS


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
scripts/push.pyGitHub 原文
"""push の入口（固定経路）。設計：local_reports/push_route_design_2026-11-13/push_route_design.md

  python -B scripts/push.py prepare   10 PLAN → 20 ASK（提示して必ず終わる）
  python -B scripts/push.py run       30 PUSH → 40 VERIFY → 50 DONE（提示の直後の利用者の入力が git push のとき）
  python -B scripts/push.py decline   提示の直後の利用者の入力が git push 以外のとき（STOP、記録の step は 20）

最後の1行だけを読む（STOP のときは直前の reason: 行もそのまま写す）。言い換えない。最初の語が呼び出し元への戻りの語（ASK・STOP・DONE）。
経路番号は最後の行に出さない（呼び出し元の番号として読まれないように）。番号は記録の step に残す。
  ASK                提示した。利用者の入力が git push なら run へ
  STOP <send_state>  止まった。send_state で送信状態が分かる（UNKNOWN と COMMAND_SUCCEEDED_UNVERIFIED は再送しない）
  DONE               完了

分岐は1つだけ：20 の利用者の入力（git push か、それ以外か）。30 の時刻差を含め、それ以外の結果はすべて STOP。
STOP の後に 10 へ自動で戻らない。やり直すときは、利用者の新しい入力で prepare から始める。
止まった場所（step）・detail・送信状態は記録（local_reports/push_gate/push_route_log.jsonl）に残し、後から追う。reason は STOP の直前の行にも出す。
send_state：NOT_STARTED（送っていない）／UNKNOWN（送ったか不明）／COMMAND_SUCCEEDED_UNVERIFIED（push は成功、照合前）／VERIFIED。

この入口は push を許可する仕組みではない。利用者の確認（git push の入力）を機械的に確かめるのは段階2。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime
import json
import os
import pathlib
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
GATE_DIR = ROOT / "local_reports" / "push_gate"
STATE = GATE_DIR / "push_route_state.json"
LOG = GATE_DIR / "push_route_log.jsonl"
TIMEOUT = {"10": 240, "30": 600, "40": 60}  # 案（設計案 U-6：実測して決める）
# 名前・パスの長さの上限。送る側では通っても、受け取る側の clone で失敗するのを先に止める。
MAX_NAME_BYTES = 255   # 名前1つの UTF-8 バイト数（Linux・macOS のファイルシステムの上限）
MAX_PATH_CHARS = 200   # リポジトリ内パスの文字数（clone 先が60文字までなら Windows の260文字に収まる）
PREPARE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 180


def _run(args, timeout, env=None):
    """(終了コード, 出力) を返す。上限時間を超えたら終了コード None。"""
    try:
        done = subprocess.run(args, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, timeout=timeout, env=env)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return None, ""
    text = (done.stdout or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace") + (done.stderr or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")
    return done.returncode, text


RUN = _run  # 試験で差し替える


def _log(step, result, reason="", detail="", send_state="NOT_STARTED"):
    GATE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rec = {"at": datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
           "step": step, "result": result, "reason": reason, "send_state": send_state}
    if detail:
        rec["detail"] = detail
    with LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")


def _stop(step, reason, detail="", send_state="NOT_STARTED"):
    _log(step, "STOP", reason, detail, send_state)
    if STATE.exists():
        STATE.unlink()
    print(f"reason: {reason}")  # 固定の語だけ。detail（パス等）は出さず記録にだけ残す
    print(f"STOP {send_state}")
    return 1


def _json_in(text):
    """出力の中の最初の { から最後の } までを JSON として読む。読めなければ {}。"""
    i, j = text.find("{"), text.rfind("}")
    if i < 0 or j < i:
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(text[i:j + 1])
    except ValueError:
        return {}


def _git(args, timeout):
    code, out = RUN(["git", *args], timeout)
    return code, out.strip()


def _new_plan_path():
    stamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    path = GATE_DIR / f"plan_{stamp}.json"
    n = 2
    while path.exists():
        path = GATE_DIR / f"plan_{stamp}_{n}.json"
        n += 1
    return path


def _too_long_path(base, head):
    """送信範囲で追加・変更・名前変更されたパスのうち、上限を超える最初のものを返す。なければ ""。"""
    code, out = RUN(["git", "diff", "--name-only", "--diff-filter=AMR", "-z", base, head], TIMEOUT["10"])
    if code != 0:
        return "DIFF_FAILED"
    for path in (p for p in out.split("\0") if p.strip()):
        name = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
        if len(name.encode("utf-8")) > MAX_NAME_BYTES or len(path) > MAX_PATH_CHARS:
            return path
    return ""


# ---- 10 PLAN ----------------------------------------------------------------
def step10(remote, branch):
    code, _ = _git(["fetch", "--quiet", remote], TIMEOUT["10"])
    if code != 0:
        return _stop("10", "TIMEOUT" if code is None else "FETCH_FAILED")
    plan = _new_plan_path()
    GATE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    code, out = RUN([sys.executable, "-B", "scripts/push_gate.py", "prepare", "--remote", remote,
                     "--branch", f"refs/heads/{branch}", "--output", plan.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(),
                     "--timeout-seconds", str(PREPARE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)], TIMEOUT["10"])
    if code is None:
        return _stop("10", "TIMEOUT")
    data = _json_in(out)
    if code != 0 or "context" not in data:
        return _stop("10", data.get("reason", "PREPARATION_FAILED"))
    ctx = data["context"]
    if not ctx.get("outgoing"):
        return _stop("10", "NOTHING_TO_SEND")
    too_long = _too_long_path(ctx.get("base_oid") or ctx["remote_oid"], ctx["source_oid"])
    if too_long == "DIFF_FAILED":
        return _stop("10", "DIFF_FAILED")
    if too_long:
        return _stop("10", "PATH_TOO_LONG", too_long)
    STATE.write_text(json.dumps({"plan": plan.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix(), "remote": remote, "branch": branch,
                                 "head": ctx["source_oid"]}, ensure_ascii=False),
                     encoding="utf-8")
    _log("10", "PLAN", plan.name)
    return step20(remote, branch, ctx, plan)


# ---- 20 ASK -----------------------------------------------------------------
def step20(remote, branch, ctx, plan):
    print(f"送信先: {remote} refs/heads/{branch}")
    print(f"HEAD: {ctx['source_oid']}")
    print(f"送信先の現在: {ctx['remote_oid']}")
    print(f"送信コミット（{len(ctx['outgoing'])}件、古い順）:")
    for oid in ctx["outgoing"]:
        _, subject = _git(["log", "-1", "--format=%s", oid], TIMEOUT["10"])
        print(f"  {oid} {subject}")
    print("incoming: 0")
    print(f"plan: {plan.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()}")
    _log("20", "ASK", plan.name)
    print("ASK")
    return 0


# ---- 30 PUSH → 40 VERIFY → 50 DONE -------------------------------------------
def run():
    if not STATE.exists():
        return _stop("30", "NO_PREPARED_PLAN")
    st = json.loads(STATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    env = dict(os.environ, NRA_PUSH_PLAN=st["plan"])
    code, out = RUN(["git", "push", "--no-progress", st["remote"], st["branch"]], TIMEOUT["30"], env=env)
    if code is None:
        return _stop("30", "TIMEOUT", send_state="UNKNOWN")  # 送ったか不明
    if code != 0:
        # ゲートの判定（JSON の reason）があれば送る前の拒否。判定がなければ、送ったか確定できない。
        reason = _json_in(out).get("reason")
        if reason:
            return _stop("30", reason)  # 時刻差（PLAN_OR_RULES_CHANGED 等）も含め、10 へは戻らない
        return _stop("30", "PUSH_FAILED", send_state="UNKNOWN")
    _log("30", "PASS", send_state="COMMAND_SUCCEEDED_UNVERIFIED")
    return step40(st)


def step40(st):
    sent = "COMMAND_SUCCEEDED_UNVERIFIED"
    code, _ = _git(["fetch", "--quiet", st["remote"]], TIMEOUT["40"])
    if code != 0:
        return _stop("40", "TIMEOUT" if code is None else "FETCH_FAILED", send_state=sent)
    ref = f"{st['remote']}/{st['branch']}"
    code, remote_oid = _git(["rev-parse", ref], TIMEOUT["40"])
    if code != 0:
        return _stop("40", "TIMEOUT" if code is None else "OBSERVE_FAILED", send_state=sent)
    code, counts = _git(["rev-list", "--left-right", "--count", f"{ref}...HEAD"], TIMEOUT["40"])
    if code != 0:
        return _stop("40", "TIMEOUT" if code is None else "OBSERVE_FAILED", send_state=sent)
    if remote_oid != st["head"] or counts.split() != ["0", "0"]:
        return _stop("40", "MISMATCH", send_state=sent)
    _log("40", "VERIFY", send_state="VERIFIED")
    STATE.unlink()
    _log("50", "DONE", send_state="VERIFIED")
    print("DONE")
    return 0


def main(argv=None):
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")  # Windows のパイプ出力（cp932）で日本語が化けないように
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="push の入口（固定経路）")
    parser.add_argument("stage", choices=["prepare", "run", "decline"])
    parser.add_argument("--remote", default="origin")
    parser.add_argument("--branch", default=None, help="既定は現在のブランチ")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.stage == "run":
        return run()
    if args.stage == "decline":
        return _stop("20", "DECLINED")
    branch = args.branch
    if not branch:
        code, branch = _git(["branch", "--show-current"], TIMEOUT["10"])
        if code != 0 or not branch:
            return _stop("10", "NO_CURRENT_BRANCH")
    return step10(args.remote, branch)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
scripts/push_gate.pyGitHub 原文
"""Reviewed-target checks for pre-push. A plan is data, never human approval.

No fetch, commit, push, broker callback, or approval issuance is performed here.
The caller still follows AGENTS.md's fresh-fetch and final-approval procedure.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid
import unicodedata

RULE_FILES = ("AGENTS.md", ".githooks/pre-push", "scripts/push_gate.py",
              "scripts/malware_gate.py", "scripts/malware_gate.ps1", "scripts/unicode_gate.py",
              "scripts/id_gate.py", "scripts/check_dictionary.py", "scripts/fileop_gate.py",
              "unicode_gate_policy.json",
              "unicode_gate_baseline.json", "id_gate_policy.json", "id_gate_baseline.json")
GATES = ("unicode", "malware", "id", "dictionary", "fileop")
ACCEPTED = {"PASS", "WARN", "NOT_APPLICABLE"}


class GateError(ValueError):
    pass


def digest(value):
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False).encode()).hexdigest()


def git(repo, *args, absent=False):
    # Never echo raw Git arguments/stderr: remote URLs may contain credentials.
    result = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(repo), *args], capture_output=True, timeout=30)
    if result.returncode:
        if absent and result.returncode == 1:
            return b""
        raise GateError("GIT_OBSERVATION_FAILED")
    return result.stdout


def inside(repo, path):
    path = Path(path)
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = repo / path
    resolved = path.resolve()
    if repo not in resolved.parents or path.is_symlink():
        raise GateError("PATH_OUTSIDE_REPOSITORY_OR_LINK")
    return resolved


def oid(repo, ref):
    value = git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", ref + "^{commit}").strip().decode("ascii")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})", value):
        raise GateError("INVALID_COMMIT_OID")
    return value


def event(repo, remote, text):
    lines = [line for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    if len(lines) != 1:
        raise GateError("SINGLE_REF_REQUIRED")
    fields = lines[0].split()
    if len(fields) != 4:
        raise GateError("MALFORMED_PRE_PUSH_INPUT")
    local_ref, source, target, old = fields
    if not re.fullmatch(r"(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})", source) or not re.fullmatch(r"(?:[0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})", old):
        raise GateError("INVALID_UPDATE_OID")
    if set(source) == {"0"}:
        raise GateError("remote ref deletion is blocked")
    if not target.startswith("refs/heads/"):
        raise GateError("new non-branch refs or non-branch updates are blocked")
    git(repo, "check-ref-format", target)
    if source != oid(repo, "HEAD"):
        raise GateError("SOURCE_MUST_EQUAL_HEAD")
    if set(old) != {"0"}:
        result = subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(repo), "merge-base", "--is-ancestor", old, source], capture_output=True, timeout=30)
        if result.returncode:
            raise GateError("non-fast-forward or unresolved remote base")
    else:
        try:
            git(repo, "symbolic-ref", "--quiet", f"refs/remotes/{remote}/HEAD")
        except GateError:
            raise GateError("new branch requires a fetched remote default branch") from None
    return local_ref, source, target, old


def context(repo, remote, target, timeout_seconds):
    if type(timeout_seconds) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(timeout_seconds) or timeout_seconds <= 0:
        raise GateError("EXPLICIT_POSITIVE_TIMEOUT_REQUIRED")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*", remote):
        raise GateError("NAMED_REMOTE_REQUIRED")
    if not target.startswith("refs/heads/"):
        raise GateError("BRANCH_TARGET_REQUIRED")
    git(repo, "check-ref-format", target)
    if Path(git(repo, "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel").decode().strip()).resolve() != repo:
        raise GateError("REPOSITORY_ROOT_REQUIRED")
    urls = git(repo, "remote", "get-url", "--push", "--all", remote).splitlines()
    if len(urls) != 1 or not urls[0]:
        raise GateError("SINGLE_DESTINATION_REQUIRED")
    head = oid(repo, "HEAD")
    tracked = f"refs/remotes/{remote}/{target[len('refs/heads/'):]}"
    old = git(repo, "rev-parse", "--verify", "--quiet", tracked, absent=True).strip().decode("ascii")
    default = None
    if old:
        base = oid(repo, tracked)
    else:
        default_ref = git(repo, "symbolic-ref", "--quiet", f"refs/remotes/{remote}/HEAD").decode().strip()
        default = oid(repo, default_ref)
        base = git(repo, "merge-base", default, head).decode().strip()
    incoming = git(repo, "rev-list", f"{head}..{old or default}").splitlines()
    if incoming:
        raise GateError("INCOMING_COMMITS_PRESENT")
    outgoing = git(repo, "rev-list", "--reverse", f"{base}..{head}").decode().splitlines()
    fetch_path = inside(repo, git(repo, "rev-parse", "--git-path", "FETCH_HEAD").decode().strip())
    fetch_raw = fetch_path.read_bytes()
    fetched_oids = {line.split(b"\t", 1)[0].decode("ascii") for line in fetch_raw.splitlines() if line}
    if (old or default) not in fetched_oids:
        raise GateError("FETCH_EVIDENCE_MISSING")
    rules = {}
    for name in RULE_FILES:
        path = inside(repo, name)
        rules[name] = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    return dict(schema_version=1, repo=str(repo), remote=remote,
                destination_sha256=hashlib.sha256(urls[0]).hexdigest(),
                remote_ref=target, head_oid=head, source_oid=head, remote_oid=old or "0" * len(head),
                base_oid=base, default_oid=default, outgoing=outgoing,
                fetch_sha256=hashlib.sha256(fetch_raw).hexdigest(), fetched_at_ns=fetch_path.stat().st_mtime_ns,
                rules=rules, timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
                python_executable=sys.executable, python_version=sys.version,
                unicode_database_version=unicodedata.unidata_version)


def prepare(repo, remote, target, output, timeout_seconds):
    ctx = context(repo, remote, target, timeout_seconds)
    plan = {"plan_id": digest(ctx), "context": ctx, "is_execution_permission": False}
    path = inside(repo, output)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    # A reviewed plan is not silently overwritten by a later preparation.
    with path.open("x", encoding="ascii", newline="\n") as handle:
        json.dump(plan, handle, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
        handle.write("\n")
    return plan


def load_plan(repo, path):
    def pairs(items):
        result = {}
        for key, value in items:
            if key in result:
                raise GateError("DUPLICATE_JSON_KEY")
            result[key] = value
        return result
    plan = json.loads(inside(repo, path).read_text(encoding="ascii"), object_pairs_hook=pairs)
    if set(plan) != {"plan_id", "context", "is_execution_permission"} or plan["is_execution_permission"] is not False:
        raise GateError("INVALID_PLAN_SCHEMA")
    if plan["plan_id"] != digest(plan["context"]):
        raise GateError("PLAN_DIGEST_MISMATCH")
    return plan


def unchanged(repo, plan):
    ctx = plan["context"]
    current = context(repo, ctx["remote"], ctx["remote_ref"], ctx["timeout_seconds"])
    if current != ctx:
        raise GateError("PLAN_OR_RULES_CHANGED")


def command(repo, args, timeout):
    try:
        return subprocess.run(args, cwd=repo, capture_output=True, timeout=timeout)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        raise GateError("GATE_TIMEOUT; task output may require cleanup") from None


def payload_result(result, expected_head, expected_base=None):
    data = json.loads(result.stdout.decode("utf-8-sig"))
    if data.get("schema_version") != 1 or data.get("head") != expected_head:
        raise GateError("INVALID_GATE_RESULT_CONTEXT")
    if expected_base is not None and data.get("base") != expected_base:
        raise GateError("INVALID_GATE_RESULT_BASE")
    status = data.get("status")
    if type(data.get("scanned_files")) is not int or data["scanned_files"] < 0:
        raise GateError("INVALID_GATE_COVERAGE")
    findings = data.get("findings")
    if not isinstance(findings, list) or status not in {"PASS", "FAIL", "UNAVAILABLE"}:
        raise GateError("INVALID_GATE_RESULT")
    if (result.returncode == 0 and (status != "PASS" or findings)) or (result.returncode == 1 and (status != "FAIL" or not findings)) or result.returncode not in (0, 1):
        raise GateError("GATE_EXIT_RESULT_MISMATCH")
    if data["scanned_files"] == 0 and status == "PASS":
        data["status"] = "NOT_APPLICABLE"
        data["reason"] = "NO_TARGETS_IN_COMMIT"
    return data


def check(repo, remote, remote_url, text, plan_path):
    results = {name: {"status": "NOT_RUN", "scanned_files": 0} for name in GATES}
    report = {"schema_version": 1, "status": "UNAVAILABLE", "results": results,
              "is_execution_permission": False}
    run_dir = None
    try:
        if not text.strip():
            report.update(status="NOT_APPLICABLE", reason="NO_REF_UPDATES")
            return report
        _, source, target, old = event(repo, remote, text)
        if not plan_path:
            raise GateError("PLAN_REQUIRED")
        plan = load_plan(repo, plan_path)
        ctx = plan["context"]
        if (remote, target, source, old) != (ctx["remote"], ctx["remote_ref"], ctx["source_oid"], ctx["remote_oid"]):
            raise GateError("UPDATE_DIFFERS_FROM_PLAN")
        if hashlib.sha256(remote_url.encode()).hexdigest() != ctx["destination_sha256"]:
            raise GateError("DESTINATION_DIFFERS_FROM_PLAN")
        unchanged(repo, plan)
        report.update(plan_id=plan["plan_id"], remote=remote, remote_ref=target, head=source, base=ctx["base_oid"])
        run_dir = inside(repo, "local_reports/push_gate/" + uuid.uuid4().hex)
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
        receipt = run_dir / "malware_unicode.json"
        result = command(repo, ["pwsh", "-NoProfile", "-File", "scripts/malware_gate.ps1",
                               "-Repository", str(repo), "-Base", ctx["base_oid"], "-Head", source,
                               "-ResultPath", str(receipt)], ctx["timeout_seconds"])
        data = json.loads(receipt.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        if data.get("schema_version") != 1 or data.get("head") != source or data.get("base") != ctx["base_oid"] or data.get("exit_code") != result.returncode:
            raise GateError("INVALID_MALWARE_RECEIPT")
        for name in ("unicode", "malware"):
            item = data[name]
            if item.get("status") not in ACCEPTED | {"FAIL", "UNAVAILABLE", "NOT_RUN"} or type(item.get("scanned_files")) is not int or item["scanned_files"] < 0:
                raise GateError("INVALID_MALWARE_COVERAGE")
            if item["status"] == "NOT_APPLICABLE" and item["scanned_files"] != 0:
                raise GateError("INVALID_EMPTY_SCOPE")
            if item["status"] in ACCEPTED:
                if type(item.get("expected_files")) is not int or item["expected_files"] != item["scanned_files"]:
                    raise GateError("INCOMPLETE_GATE_COVERAGE")
                if item["status"] in {"PASS", "WARN"} and item["scanned_files"] == 0:
                    raise GateError("ZERO_SCAN_REQUIRES_EXPLICIT_EMPTY_SCOPE")
            results[name] = item
        if result.returncode != 0 or any(results[g]["status"] not in ACCEPTED for g in ("unicode", "malware")):
            raise GateError("MALWARE_OR_UNICODE_BLOCKED")
        unchanged(repo, plan)
        commands = {
            "id": [sys.executable, "-B", "scripts/id_gate.py", "--repo", str(repo), "--base", ctx["base_oid"], "--head", source, "--policy", "id_gate_policy.json", "--baseline", "id_gate_baseline.json", "--json"],
            "dictionary": [sys.executable, "-B", "scripts/check_dictionary.py", "--repo", str(repo), "--head", source, "--json"],
            "fileop": [sys.executable, "-B", "scripts/fileop_gate.py", "--repo", str(repo), "--base", ctx["base_oid"], "--head", source, "--json"],
        }
        for name, args in commands.items():
            result = command(repo, args, ctx["timeout_seconds"])
            results[name] = payload_result(result, source, ctx["base_oid"] if name in ("id", "fileop") else None)
            unchanged(repo, plan)
            if results[name]["status"] not in ACCEPTED:
                raise GateError(name.upper() + "_BLOCKED")
        unchanged(repo, plan)
        report["status"] = "WARN" if any(r["status"] == "WARN" for r in results.values()) else "PASS"
    except (GateError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, subprocess.SubprocessError, KeyboardInterrupt) as exc:
        # Exceptions never include subprocess output, URLs or source content.
        report["reason"] = str(exc) if isinstance(exc, GateError) else "INTERRUPTED" if isinstance(exc, KeyboardInterrupt) else "GATE_INPUT_OR_EXECUTION_ERROR"
        if any(r["status"] == "FAIL" for r in results.values()):
            report["status"] = "FAIL"
    if run_dir:
        (run_dir / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="ascii")
    return report


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="action", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("prepare")
    p.add_argument("--repo", type=Path, default=Path.cwd())
    p.add_argument("--remote", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--branch", required=True, help="full refs/heads/... target")
    p.add_argument("--output", required=True)
    p.add_argument("--timeout-seconds", type=float, required=True, help="explicit trial limit; calibrate before production")
    p = sub.add_parser("check")
    p.add_argument("remote")
    p.add_argument("remote_url")
    p.add_argument("--repo", type=Path, default=Path.cwd())
    p.add_argument("--plan", default=os.environ.get("NRA_PUSH_PLAN"))
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        repo = args.repo.resolve()
        if args.action == "prepare":
            data = prepare(repo, args.remote, args.branch, args.output, args.timeout_seconds)
            code = 0
        else:
            data = check(repo, args.remote, args.remote_url, sys.stdin.read(), args.plan)
            code = 0 if data["status"] in ACCEPTED else 1 if data["status"] == "FAIL" else 2
    except GateError as exc:
        # GateError messages are fixed strings in this file (no output, URL or content), same as check().
        data, code = {"schema_version": 1, "status": "UNAVAILABLE", "reason": str(exc)}, 2
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        data, code = {"schema_version": 1, "status": "UNAVAILABLE", "reason": "PREPARATION_FAILED"}, 2
    print(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2, allow_nan=False))
    return code


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
scripts/unicode_gate.pyGitHub 原文
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import pathlib
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, replace
from typing import Iterable, Sequence


EXIT_PASS = 0
EXIT_FINDINGS = 1
EXIT_GATE_ERROR = 2
TEXT_EXTENSIONS = frozenset(".md .txt .py .js .mjs .cjs .ts .tsx .jsx .html .css .json .jsonl .yml .yaml .toml .ini .cfg .csv .tsv .xml .svg .sh .ps1 .sql .tex .bib".split())

BIDI_CONTROLS = {
    0x061C,
    0x200E,
    0x200F,
    *range(0x202A, 0x202F),
    *range(0x2066, 0x206A),
}
INVISIBLE_FORMATS = {
    0x00AD,
    0x034F,
    0x180E,
    0x200B,
    0x200C,
    0x200D,
    0x2060,
    0x3164,
    0xFEFF,
    0xFFA0,
}
LINE_SEPARATORS = {0x0085, 0x2028, 0x2029}
IDENTIFIER_RE = re.compile(r"[^\W\d]\w*", re.UNICODE)
SEVERITIES = {"warn", "fail"}
REQUIRED_RULES = {
    "BIDI_CONTROL",
    "BOM",
    "CONTROL_CHARACTER",
    "FORMAT_CHARACTER",
    "INVISIBLE_FORMAT",
    "INVALID_UTF8",
    "LINE_SEPARATOR",
    "MIXED_SCRIPT_IDENTIFIER",
    "NONCHARACTER",
    "NON_NFC",
    "OVERSIZED_TEXT",
    "PATH_CONTROL",
    "PATH_MIXED_SCRIPT",
    "PATH_NON_NFC",
    "VARIATION_SELECTOR",
}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Finding:
    rule: str
    severity: str
    path: str
    line: int | None
    column: int | None
    codepoint: str | None
    name: str | None
    category: str | None
    bidi_class: str | None
    escaped_context: str | None
    detail: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Policy:
    maximum_text_bytes: int
    normalization_form: str
    allowed_controls: frozenset[int]
    binary_extensions: frozenset[str]
    rules: dict[str, str]

    def severity(self, rule: str) -> str:
        try:
            return self.rules[rule]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"policy has no severity for rule {rule}") from exc


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BaselineEntry:
    id: str
    path: str
    file_sha256: str
    rule: str
    codepoint: str | None
    line: int | None
    column: int | None


def ascii_escape(value: str) -> str:
    return value.encode("unicode_escape", errors="backslashreplace").decode("ascii")


def sha256_bytes(raw: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


def baseline_entry_id(fields: dict[str, object]) -> str:
    canonical = json.dumps(
        fields, ensure_ascii=True, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
    ).encode("ascii")
    return "UGB-" + sha256_bytes(canonical)[:16].upper()


def codepoint_label(value: int) -> str:
    return f"U+{value:04X}"


def parse_codepoint(value: str) -> int:
    if not re.fullmatch(r"U\+[0-9A-Fa-f]{4,6}", value):
        raise ValueError(f"invalid codepoint notation: {value!r}")
    return int(value[2:], 16)


def load_policy(path: pathlib.Path) -> Policy:
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if data.get("schema_version") != 1:
        raise ValueError("unsupported policy schema_version")
    rules = data.get("rules")
    if not isinstance(rules, dict) or any(value not in SEVERITIES for value in rules.values()):
        raise ValueError("policy rules must map rule names to warn or fail")
    missing_rules = REQUIRED_RULES.difference(rules)
    if missing_rules:
        raise ValueError("policy is missing required rules: " + ",".join(sorted(missing_rules)))
    normalization = data.get("normalization_form")
    if normalization not in {"NFC", "NFD", "NFKC", "NFKD"}:
        raise ValueError("unsupported normalization_form")
    maximum = data.get("maximum_text_bytes")
    if type(maximum) is not int or maximum <= 0:
        raise ValueError("maximum_text_bytes must be a positive integer")
    return Policy(
        maximum_text_bytes=maximum,
        normalization_form=normalization,
        allowed_controls=frozenset(
            parse_codepoint(item) for item in data.get("allowed_control_codepoints", [])
        ),
        binary_extensions=frozenset(
            str(item).lower() for item in data.get("binary_extensions", [])
        ),
        rules={str(key): str(value) for key, value in rules.items()},
    )


def load_baseline(path: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[BaselineEntry, ...]:
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="ascii"))
    if data.get("schema_version") != 1:
        raise ValueError("unsupported baseline schema_version")
    raw_entries = data.get("entries")
    if not isinstance(raw_entries, list):
        raise ValueError("baseline entries must be a list")
    entries: list[BaselineEntry] = []
    ids: set[str] = set()
    for raw in raw_entries:
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            raise ValueError("baseline entry must be an object")
        fields = {
            key: raw.get(key)
            for key in ("path", "file_sha256", "rule", "codepoint", "line", "column")
        }
        expected = baseline_entry_id(fields)
        if raw.get("id") != expected:
            raise ValueError(f"invalid baseline entry id: {raw.get('id')}")
        if expected in ids:
            raise ValueError(f"duplicate baseline entry id: {expected}")
        if not isinstance(fields["path"], str):
            raise ValueError(f"invalid baseline path: {expected}")
        if not isinstance(fields["file_sha256"], str) or not re.fullmatch(
            r"[0-9a-f]{64}", fields["file_sha256"]
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"invalid baseline file hash: {expected}")
        approved_scope = {
            ("VARIATION_SELECTOR", "U+FE0F"),
            ("BOM", "U+FEFF"),
            ("INVISIBLE_FORMAT", "U+200D"),
        }
        if (fields["rule"], fields["codepoint"]) not in approved_scope:
            raise ValueError(f"baseline entry is outside approved scope: {expected}")
        if not isinstance(fields["line"], int) or not isinstance(fields["column"], int):
            raise ValueError(f"baseline entry requires line and column: {expected}")
        ids.add(expected)
        entries.append(BaselineEntry(id=expected, **fields))
    return tuple(entries)


def apply_baseline(
    path: str,
    raw: bytes,
    findings: Sequence[Finding],
    entries: Sequence[BaselineEntry],
) -> tuple[list[Finding], list[str]]:
    file_hash = sha256_bytes(raw)
    entry_map = {
        (entry.path, entry.file_sha256, entry.rule, entry.codepoint, entry.line, entry.column): entry.id
        for entry in entries
    }
    active: list[Finding] = []
    matched: list[str] = []
    for finding in findings:
        key = (
            path,
            file_hash,
            finding.rule,
            finding.codepoint,
            finding.line,
            finding.column,
        )
        entry_id = entry_map.get(key)
        if entry_id is None:
            active.append(finding)
        else:
            matched.append(entry_id)
    return active, matched


def character_name(character: str) -> str:
    return unicodedata.name(character, "UNNAMED")


def is_noncharacter(value: int) -> bool:
    return 0xFDD0 <= value <= 0xFDEF or (value & 0xFFFF) in {0xFFFE, 0xFFFF}


def is_variation_selector(value: int) -> bool:
    return 0xFE00 <= value <= 0xFE0F or 0xE0100 <= value <= 0xE01EF


def script_of(character: str) -> str | None:
    name = character_name(character)
    for script in ("LATIN", "CYRILLIC", "GREEK", "HEBREW", "ARABIC"):
        if script in name:
            return script
    return None


def risky_scripts(text: str) -> set[str]:
    scripts = {script for character in text if (script := script_of(character))}
    # Greek symbols are intentionally common in NRA-IDE formulas and units.
    # Until UTS #39 skeleton data is pinned, keep this heuristic narrowly
    # focused on the high-signal Latin/Cyrillic mixture.
    if {"LATIN", "CYRILLIC"}.issubset(scripts):
        return scripts
    return set()


def location(text: str, index: int) -> tuple[int, int, str]:
    line = text.count("\n", 0, index) + 1
    line_start = text.rfind("\n", 0, index) + 1
    line_end = text.find("\n", index)
    if line_end < 0:
        line_end = len(text)
    column = index - line_start + 1
    context_start = max(line_start, index - 20)
    context_end = min(line_end, index + 21)
    return line, column, ascii_escape(text[context_start:context_end])


def make_character_finding(
    rule: str, path: str, text: str, index: int, policy: Policy, detail: str
) -> Finding:
    character = text[index]
    value = ord(character)
    line, column, context = location(text, index)
    return Finding(
        rule=rule,
        severity=policy.severity(rule),
        path=ascii_escape(path),
        line=line,
        column=column,
        codepoint=codepoint_label(value),
        name=character_name(character),
        category=unicodedata.category(character),
        bidi_class=unicodedata.bidirectional(character) or None,
        escaped_context=context,
        detail=detail,
    )


def scan_path(path: str, policy: Policy) -> list[Finding]:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    escaped = ascii_escape(path)
    if unicodedata.normalize(policy.normalization_form, path) != path:
        findings.append(Finding(
            "PATH_NON_NFC", policy.severity("PATH_NON_NFC"), escaped,
            None, None, None, None, None, None, None,
            f"path is not {policy.normalization_form}",
        ))
    for component in re.split(r"[\\/]", path):
        for match in IDENTIFIER_RE.finditer(component):
            scripts = risky_scripts(match.group(0))
            if scripts:
                findings.append(Finding(
                    "PATH_MIXED_SCRIPT", policy.severity("PATH_MIXED_SCRIPT"), escaped,
                    None, None, None, None, None, None, None,
                    "risky scripts in path token: " + ",".join(sorted(scripts)),
                ))
    for character in path:
        value = ord(character)
        if value in BIDI_CONTROLS or (
            unicodedata.category(character) in {"Cc", "Cf"} and value not in policy.allowed_controls
        ):
            findings.append(Finding(
                "PATH_CONTROL", policy.severity("PATH_CONTROL"), escaped,
                None, None, codepoint_label(value), character_name(character),
                unicodedata.category(character), unicodedata.bidirectional(character) or None,
                None, "control or format character in path",
            ))
    return findings
